- **start-5-1**: Lesson 5.1: Keep Going - where to browse, query, and contribute next, plus advanced tools ([0xJagger/geo-onboarding](https://github.com/0xJagger/geo-onboarding/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/start-5-1))
- **start-a-business**: Guided business launch workflow from business planning and market research through pricing strategy, brand identity, social media presence, and financial management for new entrepreneurs starting a service-based or product-based business. Use when the user wants to start a business or needs a structured multi-step process for this goal. Do NOT use when the request is a single-step task or requires professional advice beyond educational guidance. ([FerroxLabs/wayland](https://github.com/FerroxLabs/wayland/tree/HEAD/src/process/resources/skills-library/bodies/workflows/start-a-business) · ★618 · 3 copies)
- **start-a-business**: Walk a person through starting and keeping a US business (LLC, corporation or nonprofit) in any state or DC, using each state's real filing fees, due dates and official links from the FoundIt open dataset. Use when someone asks what it costs to start a business, how to form an LLC or corporation, what a state charges each year, when an annual report is due, or how to register a business in another state. ([bragoatski/foundit-business-fees](https://github.com/bragoatski/foundit-business-fees/tree/HEAD/skills/start-a-business))
- **start-a-campaign**: Plan and create a HeyLead LinkedIn outreach campaign for a goal - find customers (lead generation, B2B prospecting), find a job (hiring managers and referrals), hire, find partners or investors, find a vendor, or recruit research interviews. Use when the user wants to reach a kind of person on LinkedIn. Creates a draft; launching is a separate, explicit step. ([D4umak/linkedin-outreach-mcp](https://github.com/D4umak/linkedin-outreach-mcp/tree/HEAD/plugins/heylead/skills/start-a-campaign) · ★13)
- **start-a-chief-of-staff-session**: Use at the start of a chat when I say "chief of staff", "CoS" or "connect to my Notion as…", or when a tool's instructions say to start every session this way. Loads the persona, the rules and the agent list, picks the mode, and gets ready to route. Not for doing specialist work itself. ([raphaelmans/notion-skills](https://github.com/raphaelmans/notion-skills/tree/HEAD/plugins/orchestration/skills/start-a-chief-of-staff-session))
- **start-a-couch-to-5k**: Build from little or no running to finishing a comfortable 5 km using gradual run-walk sessions. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-01-howto-main-daily-self-care-start-a-couch-to-5k-aeb962ce))
- **start-a-design**: Use at the start of every new design — a new part, a device, a bracket, a mechanism, an assembly, a decorative model. Asks the user (as buttons in the chat) what the design is for, whether it is 3D printed and whether it must be easy for a person to edit, then picks the skills that fit. ([FilipPaul/Auto3DModeler](https://github.com/FilipPaul/Auto3DModeler/tree/HEAD/agent_plugin/skills/start-a-design))
- **start-a-focus-session**: Trigger on "help me focus for an hour", "start a Pomodoro", "set me up for a focus session", "I need to concentrate on this", "Pomodoro or 90 minutes", "I keep sitting down and not working", "focus block", or a user returning with "done", "I got interrupted" or "didn't finish" after a session. Picks a format (Pomodoro 25/5, 52/17, 90-minute block, flowtime, plain one-hour block) by task and energy, writes a Focus Card (one task, what done looks like for this session, first action, parking lot, phone away, break plan), tells the user to set the timer because Claude cannot keep time, then runs… ([theAnirudhKumar/work-design](https://github.com/theAnirudhKumar/work-design/tree/HEAD/skills/start-a-focus-session))
- **start-a-gratitude-practice**: Create a short daily gratitude practice that notices specific good moments without denying real problems. ([JiayiV/question_mixing](https://github.com/JiayiV/question_mixing/tree/HEAD/skills/public-01-howto-main-daily-self-care-start-a-gratitude-practice-cf89bd8f))
- **start-a-new-app**: Take a new app from the person's first words to its first approved deploy - ask the kind of app and its name, make its private GitHub repository from a starter, then Apple, Google Cloud and Sentry as the app needs them, one card at a time, tag v0.1.0 and wait for the person to approve the deploy in Needs You. Use when a person asks for a new app ("make me an app that …"), in voice mode or typed, or asks to go on with one that stopped. ([agentloops-dev/agent-loops](https://github.com/agentloops-dev/agent-loops/tree/HEAD/Go/internal/agentsd/testdata/dotagents/skills/start-a-new-app) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **start-a-new-job**: Get through the first week, first payslip and probation period of a new job, including a first job, an apprenticeship or a graduate role. Use for "first day at a new job", "first week tips", "why is my first payslip so low", "what is probation", "W-4 or tax code questions" or "zero-hours contract rights".
